A compliant foot, printed using TPU-95A is also implemented for alleviating hard impacts and handling terrain uncertainties. This extended abstract aims to introduce QLAUN, a novel platform for robotics research, emphasizing the design concepts and principles that underpin its development to the academic and research communities in the field of robotics.

</details>


### [264] [Local Path Planning and Obstacle Avoidance for an Omnicopter Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03630)
*Mikolaj Helinski,Spilios Theodoulis,Mahmoud Hamandi,Abdullah Mohamed Ali,Anthony Tzes,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向全向多旋翼（omnicopter）的六自由度动态窗口法（6D-DWA），通过局部地图体素化、球形几何近似和自适应速度采样实现实时局部规划与避障，并引入上下文感知的"Agile Mode"在线调整评分权重以提升对未知障碍的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在杂乱环境中运行时，全局规划器（如RRT*）无法在控制频率下直接使用，需要高效实时的局部规划和避障方案。同时，全向多旋翼具有六自由度运动能力，需要拓展传统DWA以充分利用其机动特性。

Method: (i) 将2D-DWA扩展为6D-DWA以匹配omnicopter的六自由度运动学；(ii) 通过局部地图体素化降低环境表示复杂度；(iii) 采用紧凑球体近似飞行器几何以简化碰撞检测；(iv) 在6D搜索空间中进行自适应速度采样保证实时性；(v) 提出Agile Mode，根据场景上下文在线调整目标推进、安全间距与航向/朝向约束之间的评分权重。

Result: 在仿真中，规划器稳定运行于0.2s控制周期内；密集航点路径跟踪的平均横向误差小于0.1m，平均航向误差13度；静态环境避障成功。对于未知障碍，Agile Mode在偏心障碍场景下达到79.3%成功率，居中障碍场景为41.4%。

Conclusion: 6D-DWA方法验证了其在全向多旋翼上实现实时局部规划的可行性，Agile Mode的自适应权重机制对未知障碍避碰有效，但在高度受限几何构型下性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly operate in cluttered environments where global planners such as RRT* are not directly deployable at control rates. This paper presents a real-time local planning and obstacle avoidance module for an omnidirectional multirotor (omnicopter) by extending the Dynamic Window Approach to six degrees of freedom (6D-DWA). Our method achieves real-time feasibility through (i) local-map voxelisation, (ii) a compact sphere-based approximation of the vehicle geometry, and (iii) adaptive velocity sampling in the 6D search space. To improve reactivity to unknown obstacles, we introduce a context-aware "Agile Mode" that adjusts scoring weights online to trade-off between goal progress, clearance, and heading/facing constraints during evasive manoeuvres. We evaluate our approach in simulation across computational stress tests, dense-waypoint path tracking, and static/unknown obstacle scenarios. Our planner runs consistently within a 0.2s control loop, tracks waypoint-dense global paths with < 0.1m average cross-track error and 13deg average heading error, and avoids collisions in static environments. For unknown obstacle avoidance, Agile Mode achieves 79.3% success for an off-centre obstacle and 41.4% for a centred obstacle, highlighting both the effectiveness of adaptive weighting and remaining limitations in highly constrained geometries.

</details>


### [265] [WISE: World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03681)
*Chenhao Zhang,Hanyu Zhao,Hang Cheng,Tengfei Pan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WISE是一个用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型后训练的统一框架，通过选择性调用世界模型想象、进行有界的多视角展开以及利用进展和完成信号评估候选未来来优化策略，在π0和π0.5上实现了一致的性能提升，并将GPU计算时间减少约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略的后训练通常依赖昂贵的专家演示进行监督微调，或依赖昂贵且可能不稳定的真实世界探索进行强化学习。世界模型虽然有前景，但有效的后训练不仅需要准确的预测，还需要在有用处编排想象调度、将想象限制在可靠的视界内，并将其转化为可信的策略监督——因为机器人操纵中，不同执行阶段想象价值差异大，而长展开会累积预测误差。

Method: WISE框架通过三个关键机制协调世界模型想象的使用：(1) 在交互相关状态选择性触发想象；(2) 执行有界的多视角展开；(3) 使用进展和完成信号评估候选未来，并利用候选的相对结果来优化从真实交互语境中生成的动作。

Result: 在π0和π0.5上进行了广泛实验，结果显示在多种操纵任务中实现一致的性能提升，同时相比完全想象模式减少了约80%的GPU计算时间；真实世界评估进一步表明在多种分布偏移下具有实质性的鲁棒性和泛化能力提升。

Conclusion: WISE通过想象调度策略解决了世界模型在后训练中的效率与可靠性问题，证明了"何时以及如何使用想象"比单纯追求预测准确性更为关键，为VLA策略的高效后训练提供了新范式，并在计算效率和真实世界性能之间取得了显著权衡优势。

Abstract: Post-training VLA policies typically rely on supervised fine-tuning with costly expert demonstrations or reinforcement learning with expensive and potentially unstable real-world exploration. World models offer a promising alternative by evaluating candidate behaviors through imagined futures, yet effective post-training requires more than accurate prediction: imagination must be scheduled where it is useful, bounded within reliable horizons, and translated into trustworthy policy supervision. In robotic manipulation, the value of imagination varies substantially across execution stages, while extended rollouts can accumulate prediction errors and introduce unreliable learning signals. We introduce WISE (World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models), a unified framework that coordinates when and how world-model imagination is used during policy refinement. WISE selectively invokes imagination at interaction-relevant states, performs bounded multi-view rollouts, evaluates candidate futures using progress and completion signals, and uses their relative outcomes to refine actions generated from real interaction contexts. Extensive experiments with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse manipulation tasks while reducing GPU computation time by approximately 80% compared with full imagination. Real-world evaluations further show substantial gains in robustness and generalization under diverse real-world distribution shifts.

</details>


### [266] [Predictive Zonotope Reduction: Precise Runtime Monitoring under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03699)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Florian Kohn,Bernd Finkbeiner,Milan Simovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性Zonotope约减（PZR）方法，将运行时监控中的zonotope约减器选择建模为最优控制问题，利用波束搜索模型预测控制求解，并通过策略蒸馏得到小型神经策略提升执行速度，在RLola框架中实现对机械臂基于不确定性感知的运行时监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在物理环境中基于不确定传感器测量进行控制决策可能导致不安全或次优行为，运行时监控需合理表示这种不确定性。Zonotope虽被广泛使用，但持续纳入新测量会导致其阶数无界增长，需周期性进行过近似约减。现有方法固定使用单一约减策略，然而最优选择依赖于当前状态，导致精度不足。

Method: 该方法将约减器选择形式化为最优控制问题，采用波束搜索模型预测控制求解；随后通过策略蒸馏将MPC策略压缩为小型神经策略，以在保持性能的同时大幅提升执行速度，满足资源受限的实时系统需求。方法在RLola框架中实现，并在MuJoCo模拟的5自由度机械臂（传感器不确定性按ISO 5725建模）上评估。

Result: 在树莓派5上的实验表明，与静态约减策略相比，动态约减显著降低了监控中的假阳性率；神经策略在保持优于静态策略性能的同时，执行速度远高于模型预测控制。

Conclusion: 自适应的动态zonotope约减策略选择优于固定策略，且通过策略蒸馏可实现高效部署，为资源受限实时系统上的不确定性感知运行时监控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in physical environments make control decisions based on uncertain sensor measurements, which can lead to unsafe or suboptimal actions. Runtime monitors that check their behavior against safety specifications must represent this uncertainty soundly. Zonotopes are a widely used representation, but continuously incorporating new measurements grows their order unboundedly, so monitors must periodically apply an over-approximating reduction. The choice of the reduction method substantially affects the zonotope's precision, yet existing approaches typically utilize a fixed method throughout the run, even though the optimal choice depends on the current state. This paper presents a Predictive Zonotope Reduction (PZR) approach, which frames reducer selection as an optimal control problem and solves it using beam-search model predictive control. Policy distillation into a small neural policy further provides substantially higher execution speed than model predictive control while maintaining improved performance, enabling uncertainty-aware runtime monitoring on resource-constrained real-time systems. We implement our approach in the RLola runtime monitoring framework and evaluate it on a 5-degree-of-freedom robotic arm simulated in MuJoCo, with sensor uncertainty modeled according to ISO 5725. Experiments on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that dynamic reduction significantly lowers false-positive rates in monitoring compared with static reduction strategies.

</details>


### [267] [Toward an~Integrated Cognitive--Ergonomic Architecture for~Human--Machine Interaction: Combining Cognitive Models with~Human Factors Ergonomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03704)
*Antoine Lenat,Olivier Cheminat,Damien Chablat,Camilo Charron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种整合认知架构与人体工效学（HFE）理论的综合方法，用于建模人类能力。通过对SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM四种成熟认知模型的比较分析，合成了一种面向动态环境中人机交互复杂性的定制化架构，并在工业机器人应用中进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型与人体工效学理论各自独立发展，缺乏整合框架来弥合认知理论与应用系统设计之间的鸿沟，尤其是在动态环境中人机交互系统需要同时考虑决策机制、技能习得与自适应行为的需求日益增长。

Method: 采用比较分析方法对SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM四种认知架构进行系统对比，提取各自优势特征并合成一种定制化认知架构；将该架构置于工效学框架下进行情境化解析，阐明决策制定、技能习得和自适应行为背后的机制，并以工业机器人应用情境进行实证验证。

Result: 提出的架构有效增强了人机交互系统的认知对齐性，在操作者专业知识、规范性知识和实时反馈循环关键的工业机器人应用中表现良好，并提供了一种在高风险环境中设计智能、以人为中心界面的可扩展方法论。

Conclusion: 该工作同时推进了人类能力的理论理解与自适应、工效学优化系统的实际部署，为连接认知科学理论与工程应用提供了可行桥梁。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated approach to modeling human competencies by combining the theoretical foundations of cognitive architectures with principles from Human Factors Ergonomics (HFE). Through a comparative analysis of established cognitive models-SOAR, ACT-R, LIDA, and COCOM-we synthesize a tailored architecture designed to address the complexities of human-machine interaction (HMI) in dynamic environments. By contextualizing this model within ergonomic frameworks, we elucidate the mechanisms underlying decision-making, skill acquisition, and adaptive behavior, bridging the gap between cognitive theory and applied system design. Our framework is empirically grounded in industrial robotics applications, where operator expertise, normative knowledge, and real-time feedback loops are critical. The proposed architecture not only enhances the cognitive alignment of HMI systems but also provides a scalable methodology for designing intelligent, human-centered interfaces in high-stakes environments. This work advances both the theoretical understanding of human competencies and the practical implementation of adaptive, ergonomically optimized systems.

</details>


### [268] [MINERVA: How Small Can a Manipulation Policy Be and Still Solve LIBERO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03715)
*Kohei Sendai,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出MINERVA，一组刻意紧凑的视觉-动作（visuomotor）策略，用于量化LIBERO操作基准的任务特定容量下限。一个仅0.54M参数的策略在四套标准LIBERO套件上2000次rollout中取得95.1%的平均成功率，比LeRobot π0.5仅低2.4个点，但参数量少7,700倍。性能在约1M参数处饱和，低于0.25M则崩溃。该工作还发现动作块长度和视觉容量是唯一稳健影响性能的因素，流匹配相对于直接L1回归无优势，且标准LIBERO指令条件化主要是在记忆任务间进行选择。在LIBERO-Plus扰动下鲁棒性近乎为零，但在笔记本电脑CPU上推理速度较现有模型快113–1,400倍，建立了LIBERO任务特定容量下限的首个经验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的VLA模型以数十亿参数主导LIBERO操作基准，但该基准实际所需的模型容量并不清楚。作者旨在通过构建刻意紧凑的策略家族来精确测量LIBERO任务特定的容量下限，从而揭示常见的大规模模型是否在该基准上过度参数化，并为部署高效的机器人策略设计提供依据。

Method: MINERVA是一族刻意紧凑的视动策略，通过系统性地缩减参数规模来探测容量下限。作者在多个维度（架构、训练、推理）进行广泛消融，分析动作块长度、视觉容量及流匹配vs L1回归等因子的影响；使用任务ID排列探针揭示指令条件化的本质；并在不同的LIBERO变体（标准套件、LIBERO-90、LIBERO-Plus）上评估泛化与鲁棒性，同时在笔记本电脑CPU上测量推理延迟。

Result: 0.54M参数策略在LIBERO四套件上达到95.1%平均成功率（仅比π0.5低2.4个点，参数量少7,700倍）；性能在~1M参数处饱和，低于0.25M崩溃；动作块长度和视觉容量是唯一超出±1点训练种子带的影响因素；流匹配相对L1回归无优势且慢达3.8倍；任务ID排列探针表明LIBERO指令条件化主要是在记忆任务间选择；LIBERO-90上达94.6%但LIBERO-Plus仅46–56%，对光度扰动近乎零鲁棒；推理速度113–1,400倍于现有模型。

Conclusion: LIBERO基准存在一个远低于当前VLA模型规模的任务特定容量下限，约~1M参数即可饱和。该发现表明大规模VLA模型在该基准上严重过度参数化，同时也暴露了LIBERO-Plus扰动下极低的鲁棒性。这项工作推动容量感知的设计和蒸馏策略，以实现部署高效的机器人策略。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models with billions of parameters now dominate the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, but the model capacity actually required by the benchmark remains unclear. We introduce MINERVA (MINimal Efficient Robotic Vision-Action policy), a family of deliberately compact visuomotor policies designed to measure this task-specific capacity floor. A 0.54M-parameter policy achieves 95.1% average success over 2,000 rollouts on the four standard LIBERO suites, only 2.4 points below the reported LeRobot $π_{0.5}$ result despite using 7,700$\times$ fewer parameters. Performance saturates near 1M parameters and collapses below 0.25M. Across broad architectural, training, and inference sweeps, only action-chunk length and vision capacity consistently exceed a $\pm$1-point training-seed band. Flow matching provides no detectable advantage over direct L1 regression across three seeds, while regression is up to 3.8$\times$ faster on GPU. A task-ID permutation probe shows that standard LIBERO instruction conditioning primarily selects among memorized tasks: changing only the task-ID mapping reduces success to near chance. The same recipe achieves 94.6% success across 89 LIBERO-90 tasks, while LIBERO-Plus perturbations reduce performance to 46--56%, with near-zero robustness to photometric shifts. The 0.54M policy replans every control step in 5--9 ms per chunk on a laptop CPU, 113$\times$ faster than SmolVLA and 1,400$\times$ faster than $π_{0.5}$, without a GPU. These results establish a first empirical estimate of LIBERO's task-specific capacity floor and motivate capacity-aware design and distillation for deployment-efficient robot policies.

</details>


### [269] [RoughSense: Lightweight Terrain-Induced Rover Vibration Prediction Using Point Clouds and IMU Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03720)
*Gabriel Manuel Garcia,Stephanie Aravecchia,Miguel Angel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的实时振动感知可通行性映射方法，利用LiDAR点云和IMU数据，通过RANSAC估计地形几何振动代理，并结合递推最小二乘法在线修正，在月球模拟环境、户外场地和地下矿井中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间导航（特别是地下环境）需要可靠的地形评估以确保安全操作，但此类环境通信、计算资源和功耗预算都极为有限，因此需要轻量级的实时可通行性评估方法。

Method: 该方法包含两条并行通路：(1) 基于SLAM产生的局部点云patch，利用RANSAC估计初始振动代理（来自地形几何）；(2) IMU提供漫游车行驶过程中实际经历的振动观测。随后通过递推最小二乘法（RLS）在线校正基于点云的地形几何预测，使其适应实测的车辆响应。

Result: 该方法在三种不同环境中进行了评估：月球模拟环境、户外场地以及地下矿井，验证了其在多样地形条件下的有效性和实时性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在计算和功耗受限的条件下，结合几何先验与实测振动响应，自适应地生成可靠的可通行性地图，适用于地下等通信受限空间中的自主导航任务。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in space requires reliable terrain assessment for safe operations, especially in underground environments with limited communication, computing resources, and power budget. This paper presents a lightweight method for real-time vibration-aware traversability mapping using a Light Detecting And Ranging (LiDAR) point cloud and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements. An initial vibration proxy is estimated from terrain geometry by applying Random sample consensus (RANSAC) to local point-cloud patches produced by a Simultaneous Localisation And Mapping (SLAM) algorithm. In parallel, the IMU provides local observations of the vibration experienced by the rover during traversal. The point-cloud-based prediction is then corrected online using Recursive Least Squares, allowing the system to adapt the geometric estimate to the measured rover response. The approach is evaluated in a lunar analogue environment, an outdoor field, and an underground mine.

</details>


### [270] [A Multi-Vine Soft Robot Enabling Accessible Working Channel and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03758)
*Reza Kashef,Cem Suulker,Mohammad Sheikh Sofla,Kaspar Althoefer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多藤蔓（multi-vine）机器人架构，将两段藤蔓机器人通过软安装尖端与外部集成工作通道耦合，利用独立藤蔓驱动实现生长过程中的主动尖端转向（接近90度），克服了传统藤蔓机器人难以通过锐角路径和工具输送受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体外翻式（vine）机器人在导航和微创医疗中具有低摩擦生长优势，但在结肠乙状结肠等锐角转弯处被动生长受限，需要主动转向；同时现有将工作通道嵌入机器人本体的方案限制工具尺寸、引入摩擦且仅能触及尖端环境。因此需要一种兼具主动转向能力和工具输送能力的架构。

Method: 提出多藤蔓架构：将两个藤蔓机器人通过软安装尖端与一个外部集成工作通道耦合并联配置。两个藤蔓独立驱动加压，通过差动驱动实现工作通道的主动弯曲转向，同时工作通道不嵌入藤蔓本体内部，可随藤蔓生长推进并持续暴露于外部环境。

Result: 实验验证该架构在生长过程中实现了接近90度的锐角主动转向，展示了该多藤蔓构型在复杂弯曲路径下的可行性与潜力。

Conclusion: 多藤蔓耦合工作通道的架构为藤蔓机器人提供了一种兼具低摩擦生长、主动锐角转向及外部工具输送能力的解决方案，有望用于ERCSynergy项目EndoTheranostics中微创手术治疗，并具有广泛的医疗与非医疗应用前景。

Abstract: Soft eversion robots, also known as vine robots, have attracted growing interest for navigation and inspection tasks, including minimally invasive medical applications [1]. A vine robot consists of a thin, flexible, inextensible tube folded inward that everts and grows forward when pressurized. This tip-growth enables navigation with minimal friction, making vine robots well suited for complex environments such as the human colon [2]. While their inherent softness allows passive conforma- tion to curved pathways in confined spaces, navigation performance strongly depends on environmental inter- actions, including contact angle and the length of un- constrained deployed material [3], [4]. Sharp directional changes, such as those in the sigmoid colon, often limit passive growth and necessitate active steering. Existing solutions include distributed artificial muscles [5] or dedicated tip-based steering mechanisms [6]. In addition, many applications require payload delivery, such as sensors and tools [7], [8]. Within the ERC Synergy project EndoTheranostics, this motivates the development of vine robots capable of delivering micro- surgical tools during growth. Prior work has integrated working channels within the vine body [8], [9], but these approaches constrain tool size, introduce friction, and limit access to the environment to the robot tip. In this work, we propose a multi-vine architecture in which two vine robots are coupled to an externally integrated working channel via soft mounting tips [10]. Independent vine actuation enables active tip steering while advancing the working channel without embed- ding it within the vine bodies Figure 1. Experiments demonstrate sharp steering of nearly 90 degrees during growth, highlighting the potential of this architecture for versatile medical and non-medical applications.

</details>


### [271] [Virtual Testing of Automated Driving Systems through Credible Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03760)
*Riccardo Dona,Espedito Rusciano,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险的仿真工具链可信度评估框架，用于自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中的虚拟测试（VT）。该框架借鉴NASA STD-7009标准，将传统V&V扩展为包含工具链管理、建模假设、验证、确认和敏感性分析的生命周期导向评估方案，并根据仿真用途（探索性分析、部分决策支持或替代物理测试）按比例设定差异化可信度验收阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统行为的复杂性和操作设计域（ODD）的规模使得完全依赖物理测试不切实际，虚拟测试（VT）在审批阶段被广泛使用。然而，当前ADS领域的VT认证方法通常仅依赖验证（validation-only）实践，这在复杂的多工具仿真环境中扩展性差，无法有效支撑道路安全决策的仿真结果可信度评估。

Method: 提出了一个基于风险的可信度评估框架，借鉴NASA STD-7009（航空航天领域针对仿真模型和仿真的可信度评估标准），扩展传统的验证与确认（V&V）。框架将可信度要求与仿真输出的预期用途及审批过程中所支持决策的安全关键性明确关联，提供覆盖工具链管理、建模假设与局限、验证、确认和敏感性分析的生命周期评估方案，并按使用场景（探索性安全分析、部分决策支持、替代物理测试）设定比例化的可信度验收阈值。

Result: 该框架在ADS领域得到演示验证，证明其能够根据不同仿真用途差异化地设定可信度要求。框架不仅适用于ADS，还可直接应用于以虚拟测试为核心安全评估与监管决策的道路安全和仿真研究领域。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险驱动生命周期可信度评估框架为解决复杂、多工具仿真环境中ADS审批的可信度验证瓶颈提供了可行方案，相比传统validation-only方法更具可扩展性和决策相关性。

Abstract: Simulation is increasingly used to support safety-related decision-making in road transport, particularly for the assessment and approval of automated driving systems (ADS). The complexity of ADS behavior and size of their operational design domains make exclusive reliance on physical testing impractical, leading to extensive use of virtual testing (VT) during the approval phase. This shift raises critical questions regarding the credibility of modelling and simulation (M&S) results used to support road safety decisions. Current VT accreditation approaches in the ADS domain typically rely on validation-only practices, which have been shown to scale poorly when applied to complex, multi-tool simulation environments. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a risk-based framework for assessing the credibility of simulation toolchains used in ADS safety evaluation, drawing inspiration from established practices in other safety-critical domains, notably NASA's STD-7009 for models and simulations. The framework extends traditional verification and validation (V&V) by explicitly linking credibility requirements to the intended use of simulation outputs and to the safety criticality of the decisions they support within the approval process. It provides a lifecycle-oriented assessment scheme integrating toolchain management, modelling assumptions and limitations, verification, validation, and sensitivity analysis. Credibility acceptance thresholds are defined proportionally, allowing differentiated requirements depending on whether simulation is used for exploratory safety analysis, partial decision support, or as a substitute for physical testing. While demonstrated for ADS, the proposed approach is directly applicable to road safety and simulation studies where VT plays a central role in safety assessment and regulatory decision-making.

</details>


### [272] [Robot Aware Computational Design of Object Specific Passive Grippers for Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03761)
*Abdullah Yahya Abdullah Omaisan,Ibrahim Sheikh Mohamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端计算流水线，将选定的物体网格、实测物体位姿与六轴机器人整合为具物体特异性、无驱动的增材制造夹爪。系统耦合位姿配准、表面接触采样、不确定性力螺旋筛选、被动捕获机构选择、物体适形表面合成、全姿态逆运动学、扫描体感知制造域、定向FDM有限元筛选与三维SIMP拓扑优化，每个导出设计均通过可追溯设计标识绑定到源网格、位姿、法兰、接触集与插入假设。四个存档物体（兔形件、相机法兰、3DBenchy、刻面胸像）通过了标称拟合、不确定力螺旋、运行扫掠及基线/后拓扑FEA门限；放大的+/-3mm、+/-5度位姿压力检验保留了160次模拟中的29-134次，重建拓扑保留FE域92.0-97.6%。所有设计停留在数字筛选状态而非运行发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流将抓取选择、工具几何、运动规划和结构设计视为相互分离的问题，缺乏将各环节耦合并保持每个设计全链路可追溯的统一方法。作者旨在建立一个端到端且可解释的夹爪设计流水线，使导出的设计完全绑定到源物体、位姿、机器人法兰、接触集与插入假设。

Method: 方法为多阶段耦合计算流水线，包含：精确网格RGB-D/ICP位姿配准、确定性表面接触采样、不确定性感知力螺旋筛选、六种被动捕获机构的选择、物体适形表面合成、全姿态机器人逆运动学、扫描体感知制造域、定向FDM有限元筛选以及约束三维SIMP拓扑优化。作者推导了配准、接触、拟合公差、FE与密度优化方程，并证明了节点载荷保持、SIMP插值下单调柔度灵敏度及拓扑后处理后的体素域包含性三项数值性质。

Result: 四个物体（兔形件、相机法兰、3DBenchy、刻面胸像）均通过标称拟合、不确定力螺旋、运行扫掠与基线/后拓扑FEA门限。外部+/-3mm、+/-5度位姿应力检验区分了各设计，保留160次模拟中的29-134次。重建拓扑保留92.0-97.6%的FE域，因功能区受保护。存档机器人照片定性展示打印装配体，但因仅用标称材料属性且缺乏标定仪器测试，全部停留在数字筛选状态。

Conclusion: 该流水线展现了从物体到可制造、无驱动被动夹爪的统一可追溯设计能力，但在运营发布前仍需补充基材标定及仪器化机械测试以验证实际性能。数字筛选与实物照片构成中间验证层，系统性物理试验为后续方向。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end computational pipeline that converts a selected object mesh, a measured object state, and a selected six-axis robot into an object-specific, unactuated, additively manufacturable gripper. The method couples exact-mesh RGB-D/ICP pose registration, deterministic surface-contact sampling, uncertainty-aware wrench screening, selection among six passive capture mechanisms, object-conformal surface synthesis, full-orientation robot inverse kinematics, a swept-volume-aware manufacturing domain, directional fused-deposition finite-element screening, and constrained three-dimensional SIMP topology optimization. Unlike workflows that treat grasp selection, tool geometry, motion, and structural design as separate problems, every exported design is bound to the source mesh, object pose, robot flange, contact set, and insertion hypothesis by a traceable design identifier. We derive the implemented registration, contact, fit-tolerance, finite-element, and density-optimization equations and prove three properties of the numerical construction: nodal load preservation, monotonic compliance sensitivity under SIMP interpolation, and voxel-domain containment after topology post-processing. Four archived object-specific attempts - a rabbit, camera flange, 3DBenchy, and faceted bust - meet the nominal fit, uncertain-wrench, runtime-sweep, and baseline/post-topology FEA gates. A deliberately enlarged +/-3 mm, +/-5 degree pose stress check differentiates the designs, retaining 29-134 of 160 simulated trials. Their reconstructed topologies retain 92.0-97.6% of the FE domain because functional regions are protected. Archived robot photographs show the corresponding printed assemblies qualitatively, while nominal material properties and absent coupon-calibrated, instrumented tests keep all four at digital-screening status rather than operational release.

</details>


### [273] [A comparative study on the accuracy & repeatability of mobile robotic platforms for the delivery of precision NDE measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03794)
*SeyedMohammadAmin Nabi Pour,S. Gareth Pierce,Randika Vithanage,Ehsan Mohseni,David Carswell,Matthew Shields*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于激光跟踪仪的移动机器人定位精度评估工作流（地面真值约6微米），对五种商用移动平台（KUKA KMP-1500、KUKA KMR、MiR250、Boston Dynamics Spot、Clearpath Husky）在统一协议下测量静态和分段轨迹定位精度。结果显示静态定位精度中位数从8.2 mm（KMP-1500）到63.5 mm（Spot）不等，动态路径跟随横跨误差从6.9 mm到112.1 mm。没有任何平台能满足航空NDE 0.2–1.0 mm的公差要求，最佳平台需要约一个数量级的补充传感补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人平台为大型航空结构的无损检测（NDE）提供了比固定机械臂更灵活的替代方案，但各平台的基座定位精度及其对部署策略的影响尚未在统一的外部参照协议下进行评估，缺乏可复现的平台选择依据。

Method: 采用激光跟踪仪建立评估工作流（地面真值约6微米），统一协议下测量五种商用移动平台的静态和分段轨迹定位精度。使用耦合多角点标定恢复激光-机器人变换及反射器偏移，对所有位姿采用普通最小二乘，鲁棒估计仅作为粗差检查保留。

Result: 静态定位精度中位数范围8.2 mm（KMP-1500）至63.5 mm（Spot），仅轮式里程计的Husky无法标定。动态路径跟随横跨误差对时间对齐不敏感，范围6.9 mm（KMP-1500）至112.1 mm（Spot）。精度和可标定性均与定位能力正相关（从最新LiDAR SLAM平台到无地图视觉里程计）。无平台能在基座层面达到0.2–1.0 mm的航空NDE公差。

Conclusion: 研究结果作为设计输入而非可行性声明：用于量化各平台所需的补充传感规模——最佳平台需约一个数量级的补偿，最差的需接近两个数量级。该工作为平台选择提供了可复现的科学依据。

Abstract: Mobile robotic platforms offer a flexible alternative to fixed manipulators for non-destructive evaluation (NDE) of large aerospace structures, but their base-positioning accuracy and how that accuracy should inform deployment have not been assessed under a common, externally referenced protocol. This work presents a laser tracker-based evaluation workflow (ground truth approximately 6 micrometers) that measures the static and segmented trajectory positioning accuracy of five commercial mobile platforms (KUKA KMP-1500, KUKA KMR, MiR250, Boston Dynamics Spot, Clearpath Husky) under a common protocol. A coupled multi-corner calibration recovers the laser-to-robot transformation and reflector offsets; ordinary least squares over all poses is used, with robust estimation retained only as a blunder check.
  Static positioning accuracy ranged from a median of 8.2 mm (KMP-1500) to 63.5 mm (Spot), with the wheel-odometry-only Husky uncalibratable. Dynamic path following was characterised by cross-track error; the component was insensitive to temporal alignment, which ranged from 6.9 mm (KMP-1500) to 112.1 mm (Spot). Both accuracy and calibratability tracked localisation capability, from the newest LiDAR SLAM platform to map-free visual odometry. No configuration meets the 0.2 to 1.0 mm aerospace NDE tolerance from the base alone; the results are framed as a design input that sizes the supplementary sensing each platform requires: roughly one order of magnitude for the best platform and nearly two for the worst, providing a reproducible basis for platform selection rather than a feasibility claim.

</details>


### [274] [FWBC-VLA: Force-Aware Whole-Body Compensation for Contact-Rich Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03889)
*Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Liwen Yang,Yang Li,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Dong We,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FWBC-VLA框架，将任务级VLA动作生成与低层全身补偿控制桥接，用于轮腿机器人的接触丰富型腿臂操作。核心创新是无传感器残差力矩估计器（HSR-Force），将接触估计编码为token注入VLA解码，并通过补偿生成器产生矫正动作，在擦白板和带闭门器的开关门实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型只能生成任务级动作，无法理解动作引发的物理交互；WBC策略虽能稳定机器人但无法区分任务相关接触力与外部扰动；力/力矩传感器存在额外硬件成本和集成难度。因此需要一种无需额外传感器即可感知物理交互的桥接框架。

Method: 1) 提出HSR-Force无传感器残差力矩估计器，推断接触强度及其时间变化；2) 将接触估计编码为token注入VLA动作专家解码过程，使策略感知接触开始、持续加载和释放；3) 基于WL&Arm数据集（5000+episodes）微调预训练VLA骨干；4) 将本体感觉状态、Jacobian体坐标系力估计和接触状态联合输入补偿生成器，产生矫正动作；5) 操作动作与矫正动作组合后交由WBC策略执行。

Result: 在真实世界的擦白板和带闭门器的开门实验中验证了FWBC-VLA在接触丰富型腿臂操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: FWBC-VLA通过力感知机制成功弥合了任务级语义动作生成与物理交互补偿控制之间的鸿沟，在不添加力传感器硬件的前提下实现了接触丰富场景下轮腿机器人的稳定操作。

Abstract: Contact-rich loco-manipulation requires a bridge between semantic action generation and physical interaction control. Existing Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate task-level actions from visual and linguistic observations, but cannot interpret the physical interactions induced by those actions. While the whole-body control (WBC) policy can stabilize the robot, it cannot distinguish task-relevant interaction forces from forces induced by external disturbances during manipulation. Although force/torque sensors provide direct measurements of physical interactions, retrofitting them entails additional hardware costs and substantial integration effort, particularly for platforms not designed with sensor integration in mind. To address this problem, we propose FWBC-VLA, a force-aware framework that bridges task-level VLA action generation and low-level whole-body compensation control for wheeled-legged robots. First, we introduce HSR-Force, a sensorless residual-torque estimator for inferring contact strength and its temporal variation. These contact estimates are then encoded as tokens and injected into the VLA action expert during action decoding, enabling the policy to perceive contact onset, sustained loading, and release. For loco-manipulation tasks, all parameters of the pretrained VLA backbone are fine-tuned on our WL\&Arm Dataset, which comprises more than 5,000 episodes. Moreover, the robot's proprioceptive state, the Jacobian-derived body-frame force estimate, and the estimated contact state are jointly fed into a compensation generator to produce corrective actions. The manipulation-centric actions are subsequently combined with the corrective actions and passed to the WBC policy for execution. Real-world experiments on whiteboard wiping and door opening with a door closer demonstrate the effectiveness of our FWBC-VLA in contact-rich loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [275] [A hybrid pipeline for dynamic ontology-based semantic mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03891)
*Konstantinos Dimitropoulos,Ioannis Hatzilygeroudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合式语义建图管道，结合外部标定相机（基于单应性投影的几何建图与定位）、目标检测、持久目标跟踪及本体驱动的语义更新，并利用线性回归模型修正真实世界坐标估计，从而构建动态语义世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义建图对机器人理解、操作和导航复杂环境至关重要。传统管道融合几何建图、感知、语义融合与表示，但缺乏上下文理解能力。研究者希望通过引入先验知识（如知识图谱、语义场景图）提升环境情境理解，因此提出融合多种技术的混合管道。

Method: 系统采用混合管道：(1) 通过单应性投影的外部标定相机实现几何建图与定位；(2) 结合目标检测与持久目标跟踪；(3) 采用本体驱动的语义更新机制构建动态语义世界模型；(4) 利用线性回归模型对真实世界坐标估计值进行校正；(5) 基于实时传感数据持续更新目标实例、空间属性和语义关系；(6) 选择本体作为知识表示形式，利用其层次结构、语义表达力和动态世界建模支持能力。

Result: 系统能够基于实时传感数据持续更新目标实例、空间属性与语义关系，构建具有动态性的语义世界模型，并通过线性回归与本体驱动的更新增强模型准确性和上下文理解能力。

Conclusion: 本体驱动与混合几何-语义融合方法能够为机器人提供更具上下文感知能力的动态语义建图能力，本体因层次结构和语义表达力成为适合动态世界建模的知识表示形式。

Abstract: Semantic mapping plays a crucial role in the ability of a robot to interact with objects, operate and navigate a complex environment. The most common pipeline for semantic mapping consists of geometric mapping and localization (SLAM), perception, semantic fusion and semantic representation. However, more recent works also integrate a form of prior knowledge in their application, most notably knowledge graphs or semantic scene graphs, to improve contextual understanding of the environment. In this paper, we present a hybrid pipeline for semantic mapping. Our system incorporates an external calibrated camera using homography projection for geometric mapping and localization, combined with object detection, persistent object tracking and ontology driven semantic updates to build a dynamic semantic world model. Linear regression models are also used for correction of the estimated values of real world coordinates. The system continuously updates object instances, spatial properties and semantic relations based on real time sensory data. Ontologies are selected as form of knowledge representation due to their hierarchical structure, semantic expressiveness and support for dynamic world modelling.

</details>


### [276] [Revisiting Topological Graphs for Macro Action based Closed-loop Reinforcement Learning of Vision Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03906)
*Shuhao Ye,Sitong Mao,Yuxiang Cui,Yufei Wei,Xuan Yu,Shichao Zhai,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将连续环境视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）重构为分层马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过拓扑图抽象出宏动作空间实现端到端强化学习，并引入动作感知价值头支持基于图的PPO，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习（IL）管线在VLN-CE这一闭环设置中面临瓶颈：行为克隆受分布偏移影响，DAgger在轨迹偏离时专家动作会变得模糊；而直接对微观动作空间应用强化学习则因奖励稀疏而样本效率低下。

Method: 将VLN-CE重构为分层MDP：通过将环境抽象为拓扑图，高层策略在由前沿节点构成的宏动作空间上决策；采用免训练的低层控制器作为状态转移执行器，显著压缩决策时域使闭环RL变得可行。同时提出动作感知价值头用于在动态前沿动作空间下有效评估状态价值，支撑基于图的PPO优化。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提出架构的有效性，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过宏观动作空间与分层MDP架构，成功将RL范式应用于VLN-CE任务，以行动作感知价值头的图基PPO实现了在稀疏奖励下学习导航策略的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow natural language instructions through unseen environments. Existing imitation learning (IL) pipelines struggle in this closed-loop setting: behavior cloning suffers from distribution shift, and DAgger's expert actions become ambiguous upon trajectory deviation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a natural paradigm to address this, directly applying RL to micro action spaces is sample-inefficient due to reward sparsity. To overcome this bottleneck, we reformulate VLN-CE as a Hierarchical Markov Decision Process (MDP), explicitly decoupling high-level planning from low-level control. By abstracting the environment into a topological graph, our high-level policy operates on a macro action space of frontier nodes, with a training-free low-level controller acting as its state transition, which significantly compresses the decision horizon and makes closed-loop RL tractable. To support RL optimization on the macro MDP, we propose an action-aware value head to effectively evaluate state values under the dynamic frontier action space, powering a graph-based PPO. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our architecture. Finally, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [277] [Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03927)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Ananya Hazarika,Haochen Zhang,Fan Yang,Ryo Moriyama,Wenkai Li,Yash Patel,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的机器人学习视角，按理解（表征学习）、行动（VLA模型）、推理（世界模型）三条互补轴组织现有方法，并提供了结构化的分类体系，分析各组件间的交互关系及挑战，指出问题不仅源于各组件自身局限，更源于感知、行动和推理之间缺乏集成，最终展望统一、物理基础、概率化的机器人学习未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习研究在表征学习、VLA模型和世界模型三个方向各自快速发展，但彼此孤立开发，导致系统碎片化，难以应对泛化、长时程推理规划以及在非结构化环境中的部署等挑战。缺乏统一视角来整合这些方法。

Method: 采用综述方式，依据表征学习/VLA/世界模型三条互补轴线组织现有方法，提出结构化分类体系捕获环境表征、策略学习与预测建模中的关键设计选择；除分类外还对组件间交互进行分析，讨论共性局限并归纳新兴集成趋势。

Result: 识别出机器人学习领域的核心挑战，包括不确定性量化、分布外泛化、跨本体迁移、长上下文理解与长时程规划，并论证这些挑战既源自各组件自身局限，也源于感知-行动-推理之间的集成缺失。

Conclusion: 未来应朝向统一、物理基础且概率化的机器人学习方向发展，以构建维持一致内部表征并在真实环境中支持长时间交互决策的稳健机器人系统。

Abstract: For robots to operate reliably in real-world environments, they need to perceive their surroundings, act, and reason about the consequences of those actions. Rapid progress in the domains of representation learning, VLA models, and world models has significantly enhanced the capabilities of robot learning systems, enabling robots to work in increasingly complex environments. However, these paradigms are typically developed in isolation, resulting in fragmented systems that struggle with generalization, long-horizon temporal reasoning and planning, and deployment in unstructured environments. In this survey, we present a unified perspective on robot learning by organizing the existing methods along three complementary axes: understanding through representation learning, acting through VLA models, and reasoning through world models. We introduce a structured taxonomy that captures key design choices in environment representation, policy learning, and predictive modeling, and summarize the recent progress in these domains. Beyond classifying the existing works, we analyze how these components interact, discuss common limitations, and highlight emerging trends towards more integrated systems. Through this lens, we identify the challenges in the domain of robot learning, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-distribution generalization, cross-embodiment transfer, long-context understanding, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these challenges arise not only from limitations within individual components but also from the lack of integration across perception, action, and reasoning. Building on this analysis, we outline future directions towards unified, physically grounded, and probabilistic robot learning to develop robust robotic systems that maintain consistent internal representations and support decision making over extended interactions in real-world environments.

</details>


### [278] [Automated Weld Seam Recognition and 3D Mapping for Robotic Post Processing Using Photogrammetry and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03970)
*Augustin Raju,Abilash Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉的焊缝近似定位管线，作为高精度测量前的预扫描阶段，通过多视角图像采集、语义分割、摄影测量重建和焊缝投影，减少大型工件的扫描工作量并提高数据采集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工件的高精度激光扫描或结构光传感进行完整表面扫描既耗时又生成大量无关数据，因此需要一种粗略定位焊缝的预处理方法来降低整体扫描成本和提升数据采集效率。

Method: 该方法包括四个步骤：1) 从多个视角捕捉工件图像；2) 利用语义分割从图像中识别焊缝区域；3) 使用摄影测量技术重建工件三维模型；4) 将识别出的焊缝投影到重建模型中，实现焊缝的近似定位。

Result: 该管线作为一种实验性视觉方法，验证了在焊缝精确定位之前进行近似定位的可行性，能够减少整体扫描工作量并提升数据采集效率。

Conclusion: 提出的基于视觉的焊缝近似定位方法可作为一种预扫描阶段，在机器人后处理操作（如打磨、精加工和检测）中有效缩小高精度测量范围，提升整体流程效率。

Abstract: Accurate identification of weld seam geometries is essential for automated robotic post processing operations such as grinding, finishing, and inspection. For large workpieces, complete surface scanning using high precision laser scanners or structured light sensors can be time consuming and often generates substantial amount of data that are not relevant. This paper presents an experimental vision based pipeline for the approximate localization of weld seams. This serves as a preliminary stage before high precision measurement. The proposed approach aims to reduce the overall scanning effort and data acquisition efficiency. The proposed method includes capturing images of the workpiece from multiple viewpoints, identifying weld seams from the images using semantic segmentation, reconstructing the workpiece using photogrammetry, and projection of identified weld seams into the reconstructed model.

</details>


### [279] [MulDP: Multimodal Diffusion Policy for Autonomous Quadruped Parkour Navigation across Complex Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03984)
*Kangmai Hu,Yueqi Zhang,Peng Zhai,Xiaoyi Wei,Jiabin Hu,Zhixiang Liu,Quancheng Qian,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态扩散策略（MulDP），将视觉感知与机器人本体感觉和目标任务信息结合，生成时序连贯且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，实现四足机器人自主跑酷导航，并构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND）进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管四足机器人在跑酷运动方面展现出显著敏捷性，但大多数系统的高层规划仍依赖人工干预，自主跑酷导航领域尚未被充分探索。该任务的挑战包括细粒度速度调节、长时域前瞻行为以及感知与具身执行之间的紧密耦合，亟需一种能够将多模态信息整合并生成连贯导航策略的方法。

Method: 提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），融合视觉感知、机器人本体感觉（proprioception）以及目标任务信息，通过扩散模型生成具有时间连贯性和前瞻性的导航速度指令，实现感知与具身控制的紧密耦合。同时构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），涵盖多样化的导航行为与复杂地形，用于支持模型训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验中，MulDP实现了鲁棒的长期自主导航，并能有效穿越复杂地形。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，多模态扩散策略能够通过感知与控制的紧密耦合，有效解决四足机器人自主跑酷导航中细粒度速度调节与长时域前瞻行为等关键难题，为该领域提供了可行的解决方案和基准数据集。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have demonstrated impressive agility in parkour locomotion across complex terrains. However, most systems still rely on human intervention for high-level planning, and autonomous parkour navigation remains underexplored. The key challenges include fine-grained velocity regulation, long-horizon anticipatory behaviors, and tight coupling between perception and embodied execution. To address these challenges, we propose a Multimodal Diffusion Policy (MulDP) that integrates visual perception with robot proprioception and goal information to generate temporally coherent and anticipatory navigation velocity commands, tightly coupling perception with embodied control to enable robust autonomous navigation. To support the training of MulDP, we construct the first Quadruped Parkour Navigation Dataset (QPND), a multimodal dataset that encompasses diverse navigation behaviors and complex terrains. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that MulDP enables robust long-horizon autonomous navigation and effective traversal across complex terrains.

</details>


### [280] [Adaptive Vision-Language Grasping via Composable Foundation Priors and Generalizable Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04096)
*Sixu Yan,Shikang Wang,Binhua Huang,Xuanlai Tang,Guohua Fan,Fan Huang,Haoxuan Li,Yongkang Li,Yuhan Li,Bencheng Liao,Zeyu Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Hangxin Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoboVLG，一种任务自适应的视觉-语言-抓取(VLG)框架，支持跨不同机器人手的通用抓取综合。该框架将物理抓取综合与任务依赖理解解耦，通过显式运动学映射和力闭合稳定性估计学习高效通用基础策略，并将任务理解外包给专门的基础模型模块提供可组合先验，无需重新训练抓取策略即可实现上下文自适应抓取。实验证明该框架具备高效的跨手泛化能力，可处理空间、认知和时间先验对应的三类代表性抓取挑战，且支持在杂乱动态环境中联合执行功能性抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLG方法将基础模型与端到端抓取策略紧密耦合，导致任务自适应能力受限且泛化性差；跨不同机器人手的通用抓取综合仍然是一个挑战，需要一种解耦的、可扩展的范式来将基础模型的进步直接转化为改进的抓取能力。

Method: AdaRoboVLG采用任务自适应架构：(i)学习一个高效通用基础策略，通过显式运动学映射生成和力闭合稳定性评估物理可行的抓取候选；(ii)将任务依赖理解卸载到专门的基础模型模块，这些模块提供可组合的空间、认知和时间先验，集成到抓取综合过程中，使框架具备上下文自适应能力且无需重新训练底层抓取策略。

Result: 通过大量仿真及真实实验验证：(i)基础策略学习高效且具备强跨手泛化能力；(ii)框架有效融入空间、认知和时间先验以解决三类典型抓取挑战，综合性能不逊于最先进方法；(iii)多种先验可联合运行，在杂乱动态环境中实现功能性抓取。

Conclusion: 将物理抓取综合与任务依赖理解解耦为机器人抓取提供了可扩展范式，使基础模型的未来进展能够直接转化为改进的抓取能力，而无需重新设计或重新训练底层抓取策略。

Abstract: This paper proposes AdaRoboVLG, a task-adaptive Vision-Language-Grasp (VLG) framework that supports generalizable grasp synthesis across different robotic hands. Unlike existing VLG methods that tightly couple foundation models with end-to-end grasp policies, AdaRoboVLG learns an efficient generalizable base policy that generates and evaluates physically feasible grasp candidates through explicit kinematic mapping and force-closure-based stability estimation, while offloading task-dependent understanding to specialized foundation-model modules. These modules provide composable priors that are integrated into the grasp synthesis process, enabling contextually adaptive grasp synthesis without retraining the underlying grasp policy. Through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that (i) the base policy exhibits efficient learning and strong cross-hand generalization, (ii) the framework effectively incorporates spatial, cognitive, and temporal priors to address three representative grasping challenges without compromising grasp synthesis performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, and (iii) these priors can operate jointly to enable functional grasping in cluttered and dynamic environments. These results indicate that decoupling physical grasp synthesis from task-dependent understanding provides a scalable paradigm for robotic grasping, allowing future advances in foundation models to be directly translated into improved grasp capabilities without redesigning or retraining the underlying grasp policy. Supplementary videos are available at https://adarobovlg.github.io/

</details>


### [281] [Corner Cases: Headland Coverage Path Planning for Autonomous Driving in Arable Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04103)
*Riikka Soitinaho,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的农田地头覆盖路径规划方法，通过修改多边形拐角以允许倒车转弯，从而解决传统嵌套多边形和光滑转弯方法无法完全覆盖田角的问题。与两种现有方法相比，该方法在90度及240度以上的田角处显著改善了覆盖效果，并通过真实多边形田块地图验证了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的地头覆盖路径规划方法通常采用嵌套多边形和光滑转弯策略，但这些方法在覆盖田块角落时存在不足——要完全覆盖田角必须进行倒车机动，而现有方法未考虑这一需求，导致田角覆盖不完整。

Method: 提出一种新的地头覆盖路径规划方法，其核心思路是对多边形拐角进行修改（即对拐角几何进行改造），以允许规划出包含倒车转弯的路径，从而实现田角的完整覆盖。

Result: 与另外两种已有方法进行对比，评估指标包括间隙（gap）、重叠（overlap）和越界（crossing field boundary）。结果表明，新方法在覆盖结果上具有改进，尤其是在约为90度以及240度及以上的田角区域表现更优。此外，通过多个真实多边形田块地图的案例验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过对多边形拐角的修改和引入倒车转弯机制，该新方法能够有效改善耕地地头尤其是田角区域的路径覆盖质量，且在真实田块场景中具备良好的实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a new method for headland coverage path planning for arable fields. Several earlier approaches suggest covering the headland with nested polygons and smooth turns, however, covering the field corners entirely requires manoeuvres with reversing. In the new method, the polygon corners are modified to allow a reversing turn. A comparison to two other methods considering gap, overlap, and crossing the field boundary shows an improvement in the coverage result especially in field corners of around 90 degrees, and 240 degrees and above. Applicability of the new method is shown with several examples of real polygonal field maps.

</details>


### [282] [GIFT: Guided Intermediate Feature Training via Action-Oriented Structural Supervision for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04193)
*Yupeng Zheng,Xiang Li,Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Shuai Tian,Weize Li,Linbo Wang,Chaoyue Li,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Zhongpu Xia,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GIFT（导向中间特征训练）框架，通过在机器人策略训练中引导中间特征保留几何、可供性和目标区域三种控制相关结构，弥合视觉丰富性与控制效用之间的"动作充分性差距"。该框架架构灵活，在VLA策略、直接动作世界模型和逆动力学世界模型上都验证了有效性，零样本迁移到LIBERO-Plus和RoboCasa任务中均显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言预训练和预测性世界模型为机器人策略提供了丰富的语义和动态视觉特征，但其原生的动作与视觉预测目标可能遗漏关键的物理和任务结构，同时保留了大量与控制无关的视觉冗余。这种"视觉丰富性与控制效用之间的不匹配"（即动作充分性差距）限制了策略在下游控制任务中的表现，需要引入控制相关的结构化中间特征来弥合鸿沟。

Method: GIFT框架通过三种训练时约束来引导中间特征保留控制相关结构：(1) 几何对齐（geometry alignment）编码决定运动可行性的几何信息；(2) 可供性预测（affordance prediction）编码与指令相关的实体交互位置；(3) 目标区域重建（goal-region reconstruction）将指令锚定在任务相关区域。框架架构灵活，不改变各模型原有的动作表述方式，可实例化到VLA策略、直接动作WAM（World-Action Model）和逆动力学WAM中。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus零样本迁移中，GIFT-VLA、GIFT-WAM-Fast、GIFT-WAM-IDM分别达到79.6%、72.6%和87.8%，超越对应基线4.6、12.6和5.2个百分点；在RoboCasa上分别达到61.4%、83.6%和82.3%，超越基线12.6、9.0和8.4个百分点。在关节物体任务和受未见过视觉与空间扰动的高精度真实世界操作中，收益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了一个可复用的原则：学习功能上结构化组织的中间特征可以跨模型特定的动作表述方式带来一致收益。实验结果证明这种中间特征引导策略能够有效缩小视觉表征与控制需求之间的差距，是提升机器人操作策略泛化与精度的通用途径。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training and predictive world modeling provide robot policies with rich semantic and dynamic visual features, but their native action and visual-prediction objectives may omit critical physical and task structure while retaining control-irrelevant visual redundancy. We call this mismatch between visual richness and control utility the action-sufficiency gap. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged by guiding intermediate features to preserve three control-relevant structure in robotic manipulation: geometry governing motion feasibility, affordance encoding instruction-relevant entities, and goals grounding instructions in task-relevant regions. To this end, we present GIFT (Guided Intermediate Feature Training), an architecture-flexible framework for learning intermediate features that translates these structures into training-time constraints through geometry alignment, affordance prediction, and goal-region reconstruction. We instantiate GIFT in a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, a direct-action World-Action Model (WAM), and an inverse-dynamics WAM while retaining each model's action formulation. Under zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, GIFT-VLA, GIFT-WAM-Fast, and GIFT-WAM-IDM outperform StarVLA-OFT, Fast-WAM, and Fast-WAM-IDM by 4.6, 12.6, and 5.2 points, reaching 79.6%, 72.6%, and 87.8%, respectively. On RoboCasa, the three GIFT variants reach 61.4%, 83.6%, and 82.3%, outperforming their counterparts by 12.6, 9.0, and 8.4 points, respectively. Together, these results establish learning functionally structured intermediate features as a reusable principle across model-specific action formulations, with especially large gains on articulated-object tasks and high-precision real-world manipulation under unseen visual and spatial perturbations. Project page: https://openphoenix-team.github.io/GIFT-pages.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个编码训练语料unigram分布的"无知方向"（direction of ignorance），该方向在所有四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia）中均一致出现。将最终预测状态投影到此方向可得到先验载荷因子λ，该λ在数学上对应温度化贝叶斯更新中的unigram先验指数，从而在模型间具有可比性，且大型模型在高上下文场景下先验依赖更低。该方向具有因果性，调整λ可按KL散度方向引导预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时如何做出预测是揭示其认知机制的关键。作者旨在从几何角度解释模型的先验行为，并建立可跨模型比较的量化框架。

Method: 通过在四个模型家族的多种规模（0.4B至405B）模型中对unembedding矩阵进行结构分析，识别出编码unigram先验的单一方向；将最终预测状态投影至该方向定义先验载荷因子λ，并从数学上证明该投影将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，对应温度化贝叶斯更新中unigram先验与上下文似然两个因子；进一步通过干预λ值的因果实验验证其功能。

Result: 无知方向在四个模型家族中普遍存在；λ随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降；该几何分解与温度化贝叶斯更新的数学形式严格对应，使λ在模型间可校准比较；大模型在高上下文条件下先验依赖更低；操纵λ可沿KL方向控制预测结果。

Conclusion: 语言模型的unembedding空间几何结构编码了训练语料的unigram先验，形成了一个可量化的"无知方向"。该发现为模型预测行为提供了统一的几何-概率解释框架，且具有实际干预价值，为理解大语言模型的先验与推理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [284] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重构"(equation recast)方法，将参数化算子学习重构为学习单一规范算子，通过从控制方程解析推导参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收到有效源项中，实现在新参数域上的零样本预测，并支持跨几何结构的统一建模和可监控推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯粹数据驱动的参数化算子模型需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数才能在广泛参数范围内学习解算子，且模型在训练分布之外可能静默失败，缺乏泛化能力和可靠性保障。

Method: 方程重构将参数化算子学习转化为单一规范算子的学习。参数引起的算子变化从控制方程解析推导出来，并吸收到有效源项中，从而使模型能够在新的参数范围内进行零样本预测。此外，利用收敛性损失作为重构图迭代失败的内部警告信号，实现模型推理的可监控性。

Result: 在多方参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中，方程重构支持外推预测，并能以共享规范表示整合稀疏异构数据集。在核聚变高保真托卡马克仿真中，该框架通过规范域映射在单个联合训练的算子内统一了四种设备几何结构下的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: 方程重构为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了新路径，兼具方程引导的迁移能力、数据效率以及可监控的推理过程，展示了向通用神经求解器发展的潜力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [285] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统性地研究了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习（包括联邦学习和去中心化学习），提出了针对图分布场景的分类体系，并围绕学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度进行了系统梳理，同时指出了开放挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的集中式机器学习面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制。虽然协作学习（联邦学习、去中心化学习）已被广泛研究，但大多数研究聚焦于具有规则网格结构的欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），而难以捕捉许多真实应用中由图表示的关系模式。图学习依赖消息传递机制在连接的节点间传播信息，在概念上天然适合协作环境，但图结构数据在协作设置下的机遇与挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 本文首先回顾了协作学习在欧几里得数据上的基本原理，并按三个核心维度（学习有效性、效率、隐私保护）进行组织；随后将讨论扩展至图结构数据，提出了图分布场景的分类法，描述了相关的统计异质性，并制定了标准化的问题表述和算法框架。

Result: 本文系统地整合了从欧几里得到图结构数据的协作学习这一新兴领域，提供了全面的调查框架、图分布场景分类体系，以及标准化的问题表述和算法框架，并识别了开放挑战与有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 协作学习从欧几里得数据向图结构数据的扩展是一个充满潜力的新兴领域，但目前仍面临诸多开放挑战。本文通过系统化的综述为该领域奠定了研究基础，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [286] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了全注意力和线性注意力混合架构中注意力头分配的依据问题，通过在RoPE Transformer中发现"检索头"和"位置头"的分类学，提出了基于证据的头部级混合架构设计原则，并实例化为HwH架构，以极低的线性注意力比例实现了更强的零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合全注意力(FA)和线性注意力(LA)的架构越来越流行，但两者的分配比例和方式仍是启发式的，缺乏基于证据的理论指导。作者希望通过理解RoPE Transformer中头部级功能组织，为混合架构设计提供经验基础。

Method: 提出两个干预指标：RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数）测量每个旋转频率对注意力头分布的影响，RPD（RoPE位置依赖性）分离头部对旋转变换位置调制信息的依赖程度。在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1上验证头部分类，并通过受控Transformer实验分析分类边界与训练长度位置尺度的关系，进而提出头部级混合架构HwH（NoPE FA用于全局检索，LA用于局部位置建模）。

Result: 发现检索头和位置头可由中低频率带显著区分（称为Global Positional Band, GPBand），且此边界随训练长度的位置尺度移动。HwH在FA:LA比例低于1:3时仍保持强大的语言建模和常识推理能力，同时改善检索性能并大幅增强零样本长上下文外推，优于Transformer、纯LA和层级混合基线。

Conclusion: 提出了两条原则：位置建模应仅在局部范围进行，全局访问应通过与位置无关的检索实现；两者都应在头部粒度分配并按层特异性配置。消融实验验证了原则和组件的作用，表明基于原理的混合架构设计是未来基础模型的有前景方向。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [287] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailRL（尾似然强化学习），直接优化策略在高奖励上尾（upper tail）的概率覆盖，而非仅优化平均奖励。通过将连续奖励转化为一系列二元成功事件，最大化超过随机阈值奖励的对数概率，其梯度赋予稀有高奖励轨迹更大权重，可视为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合形式，仅需修改优势函数即可集成到现有RL流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的强化学习优化平均奖励，但生成式策略中存在一个关键盲区：两个策略可能具有相同的平均奖励，却在生成稀有但高奖励采样（rollout）的概率上差异显著。随着训练和推理期间采样次数增多，保留对高奖励结果的概率质量变得至关重要。作者因此提出应直接优化这种"覆盖率"（coverage），而非仅关注期望奖励。

Method: TailRL的核心思想是：不局限于期望奖励，而是考虑其所有上尾分布——对每个奖励阈值，衡量策略超过该阈值的概率。这将连续奖励转化为一族二元成功事件。具体地，TailRL最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数似然概率。其梯度计算会对稀有高奖励轨迹赋予更高权重，并且可以解释为一系列Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。实现上仅需对优势函数（advantage function）做简单修改，即可无缝兼容现有强化学习流程。

Result: 在对象定位、迷宫导航、图形界面（GUI）定位和代码优化等多个任务上，TailRL能够利用训练阶段稀有高奖励样本避免陷入次优解，并且学到的模型在推理时增加采样数量能带来更大的性能提升，即具有更强的采样扩展性（sample scaling benefits）。

Conclusion: 平均奖励优化无法充分刻画策略对罕见高奖励结果的覆盖能力。TailRL通过优化上尾覆盖率，使策略在训练时更好地利用和保留稀有高奖励轨迹，从而在推理时通过更多采样获得更大的收益，是一种轻量且通用的生成式策略优化改进方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [288] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量（静电势及电子、空穴准费米能级），结合有限体积电流连续性残差作为物理约束进行训练。该代理模型具备尺寸泛化能力，可通过深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环筛选最优设计，其吞吐量较完整TCAD仿真高出数个数量级，并以Sentaurus Device为基准在多鳍三栅FinFET上验证了亚伏特级RMSE精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散仿真对新兴FinFET器件设计仍是核心工具，但在3D结构中计算开销随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理模型仅将固定设计参数映射到少量标量器件指标，丢失底层物理并缺乏跨几何结构与器件族系的迁移能力，亟需兼顾物理保真与计算高效的替代方案。

Method: 1) 构建物理信息图注意力网络（GAT），直接在四面体TCAD网格上操作，在每个网格节点预测静电势、电子准费米能级与空穴准费米能级三个漂移-扩散基本未知量；2) 训练目标由数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差组成，将载流子输运物理嵌入损失函数；3) 以mesh为图操作，天然具备尺寸泛化能力，训练于少鳍网格的模型可直接应用于更大的鳍阵列；4) 采用深度集成输出逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环，快速筛选大规模候选设计并仅提交最具信息量的样本进行完整仿真。

Result: 以Sentaurus Device为基准在多鳍三栅FinFET上进行验证：1) 能再现三个漂移-扩散场，每个场的RMSE低于伏特级；2) 单设计吞吐量比完整仿真器高数个数量级；3) 优势随器件尺寸增长：直接仿真过慢的大型多鳍阵列，该代理模型仍可在每秒内完成单器件推理，从而支持在直接TCAD扫描不可行的器件尺度上进行Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 该物理信息图注意力网络替代模型通过直接在TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散场并嵌入物理约束，克服了传统ML代理在物理真实性、跨结构迁移性与计算效率间的矛盾，实现了跨器件规模的Pareto前沿探索，为新兴FinFET设计优化开辟了高效可行的路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [289] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种图网络模拟器，将颗粒间接触历史直接存储在接触边上（而非节点特征或节点级记忆中），通过边上的持久记忆、注意力消息传递和 GRU 更新，配合边身份字典应对接触图动态变化，用于高效且稳定的颗粒柱坍塌长期模拟。相比 GNS 和 NMGNS，TRACE 将长期位置误差降低 31-62%、最终沉积误差降低 58-89%，并使用更少参数，同时相比 MPM 参考求解器在 2D 和 3D 中分别提速 12.2 倍和 8.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒动力学强烈依赖于颗粒间接触历史，而现有图网络模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，难以在接触形成、断裂和重排的动态过程中有效保留交互历史，导致长期模拟精度不足。

Method: TRACE 在每条接触边上维护持久记忆，通过基于注意力的消息传递和门控循环单元（GRU）更新边状态；引入边身份字典以在接触图动态变化时保持记忆的对应关系；采用物理结构化解码器分别预测法向和切向接触力，强制执行库仑摩擦极限并保证内力的等大反向性质；训练策略为单步预训练加自回归 rollout 微调。

Result: 在 2D 和 3D 颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE 产生稳定且物理一致的长期 rollout，能精确复现最终沉积几何和坍塌动能释放。与 GNS 和 NMGNS 相比，长期位置误差降低 31-62%，最终沉积误差降低 58-89%，使用更少参数且颗粒间几乎无渗透。相对 MPM 参考求解器在 2D 和 3D 分别获得 12.2 倍和 8.9 倍加速。

Conclusion: 将接触历史显式存储在边上并配合记忆保持机制，是提升图网络颗粒模拟器长期精度与物理一致性的有效途径，该设计在精度、参数效率和计算速度上均优于现有节点级记忆方案。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [290] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLIWBO（有限库输入变形贝叶斯优化），从有限平滑输入映射库中自适应选择变形来修复几何失配问题，在保持高概率收敛保证的同时提升采样效率，并通过多组实验验证其优于原始坐标GP-UCB及其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO在昂贵黑盒函数优化中表现优异，但GP-UCB等方法的收敛保证要求固定核函数。然而核函数编码的输入几何关系在原始坐标与实际目标几何不匹配时（如对数尺度超参数或局部峰值）会导致采样效率低下。输入变形可改善此问题，但已有的GP-UCB证明无法适用于固定核之外的变形场景，缺乏同时兼顾自适应变形与理论保证的方法。

Method: FLIWBO从有限平滑输入映射库中选择变形，选择规则可以是任何历史相关策略。该框架在温和假设下保留了高概率收敛保证，并显式给出库大小导致的√(N_ε)额外代价。方法通过自适应调整输入几何加速学习，同时保持理论保证的严密性。

Result: 对照诊断实验显示有限库变形能修复植入的几何失配并能识别FLIWBO的失败案例。在四组重复基准上（变形的合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参数优化），FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能逃脱使oracle变形期望改进算法失败的陷阱，并可恢复手动对数缩放的多数收益，在所有具备匹配遗憾保证的测试方法中表现领先。20维MAS设计研究进一步验证了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO为输入变形贝叶斯优化提供了首个兼具理论收敛保证和实际自适应能力的框架，在多种变形场景与基准上均展现出显著的性能提升和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [291] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）矢量空间数据表示为图结构，将旧版和新版ENC编码为图对，并将ENC更新风险的分类任务建模为图对分类问题，通过多种GNN架构进行评估，结果表明图表示方法优于传统方法，可用于自动化和改进ENC维护工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子海图（ENC）是用于海洋导航的地理空间矢量数据集，维护这些数据集具有挑战性，尤其是根据对航行安全的重要性对ENC对象变化进行分类非常关键。然而，目前尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合此任务，因此需要一个有效的自动化方案来辅助ENC维护。

Method: 将ENC中的空间对象表示为图节点，其空间和语义关系构成边；将旧版ENC和新版ENC分别编码为两个图，构建图对；将ENC更新分类任务建模为图对分类问题，评估多种GNN架构和模型配置。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示方法在ENC更新分类上优于基线方法，能够有效区分对航行安全构成关键或非关键风险的变化，提供了一种可扩展的自动化ENC维护方案。

Conclusion: 图表示结合GNN架构为ENC更新分类提供了有效的自动化途径，可显著改进ENC维护工作流程的可扩展性和效率。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [292] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGOPD提出在提示级别验证教师模型可靠性后再决定是否使用稠密监督的on-policy蒸馏，通过少量验证器打分的教师探针来评估可靠性，仅对通过检查的提示使用稠密OPD，否则使用基于验证器的GRPO。在4B和35B学生模型的数学、代码和指令跟随任务中全面超越Vanilla OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPD对所有提示统一施加稠密监督，未检查教师模型对每个提示是否可靠。由于reverse KL是mode-seeking的，一个自信但错误的教师会施加强烈而误导性的更新。现有分布代理（如熵或师生似然一致性）只能度量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果正确性。

Method: TGOPD的核心原则是：在允许稠密监督之前，应在提示级别验证教师可靠性。具体做法是：使用一小部分验证器（verifier）打分的教师探针来估计可靠性；当可靠性检查通过时将提示路由到稠密OPD，否则路由到基于验证器的GRPO。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，涵盖数学、代码和指令跟随三个领域：TGOPD在全部6个单领域设置中优于Vanilla OPD，在多领域训练下两个规模均取得更高的七个基准平均值。此外，在异步OPD中利用原本闲置的教师算力进行可靠性估计，将教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（4B单领域实测）。

Conclusion: 提示级别的教师可靠性验证是蒸馏中至关重要的环节，将教师容量用于可靠性估计不仅提升蒸馏性能，还能有效减少异步OPD中教师侧的算力浪费，实现性能和效率的双重提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [293] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对因果基础模型（CFMs）这一新兴领域进行了实用导论性介绍，涵盖了因果推断与机器学习的基础知识，并提供了示例代码和Jupyter notebooks，帮助读者理解如何将基础模型范式应用于因果推断任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要对每个新问题定制化地构建因果机制、选择估计器并训练模型，流程繁琐低效。与此同时，机器学习领域已广泛转向基础模型范式，即在大量数据上预训练一次，无需微调即可应用到新任务。这种范式转变如何在因果推断中实现，是一个值得探索的新兴方向。

Method: 本文采用实用导论方式，首先总结因果推断和机器学习所需的背景知识，然后系统介绍因果基础模型（CFMs）的概念——即通过大规模预训练获得神经网络，能够在新数据集上采用上下文学习（in-context learning）直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需更新模型参数。文章配合示例代码和Jupyter notebooks进行教学讲解。

Result: 本文作为一篇综述/教程性质的工作，系统梳理了因果基础模型的背景、核心概念与应用方式，为研究者提供了进入该领域的实用路径和入门资源（代码与notebooks）。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型将基础模型范式引入因果推断，使预训练网络能够在新数据集上直接估计因果量而无须微调，为因果推断提供了一种可扩展的通用解决方案，是该领域一个极具前景的新兴研究方向。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [294] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench是一个基准框架，用于评估可解释性方法（内部估计器/"观察者"）是否真正适合其指导的干预、控制或安全任务。核心发现是：平均准确但选择不佳行动的估计器在闭路控制、电路干预和安全管理等场景中造成实际损失，因此需要将估计准确性与行动损失分开报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性机制（mechanistic interpretability）被越来越多地用于指导激活转向、电路移除和安全监控等干预任务，但现有的评估往往只关注内部估计器的平均准确性，忽视了一个关键问题——一个平均上准确的估计器仍可能选出糟糕的行动。这种"估计准确但行动差"的错位可能导致干预失败或安全监控失效，因此需要一个统一的基准框架来衡量"观察者"是否对由其指导的任务充分。

Method: ObserverBench为每项任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数，并分别报告估计准确性和所选行动造成的损失。基于多个理论分析和实验展开：(1) 闭路控制中分析观察者误差在起始点和干预可达方向上的影响；(2) 在GPT-2-small和Qwen2.5-7B上比较成对观察者在电路干预中的预测准确性与行动损失表现；(3) 在安全分诊中使用AUROC与部署损失进行对比，跨Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it及Qwen3.5-9B APPS任务验证；(4) 还比较稀疏SAE读数与层匹配的密集控制的表现。

Result: 实验揭示了估计准确性与行动质量之间的系统性错位：成对观察者预测未见效应更准确却不总能选出更低损失的行动；针对行动损失训练的观察者则能选择更低损失的干预。在安全分诊中，完美区分违规的分数仍可能在违规成本不同时低效分配固定干预预算。AUROC对监控器的排序不同于部署损失排序，最佳信息源跨模型变化；稀疏SAE读数在Qwen面板上落后于层匹配的密集控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench通过固定任务契约、可运行基线和基于表格的提交，为以"行动质量"而非仅"估计准确性"来评估可解释性方法提供了标准化框架。该工作强调解释性研究应将下游任务的实际决策损失作为核心评估指标，而非仅停留在估计器的统计精度上。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [295] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentDDM方法，通过局部子域预训练神经算子并在新场景下仅训练轻量级组合模块（冻结预训练算子）来实现高效迁移，在两个物理模拟任务上相比容量匹配的全域模型取得了显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理模拟的快速可微替代模型，其精度在域几何、尺寸或工况偏离训练分布时往往显著下降。虽然有监督适应可以恢复精度，但即使很小的目标数据集也需要昂贵的高保真模拟成本。因此需要设计一套预训练与迁移策略协同的方法，以降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM先在小的子域上预训练神经算子以预测物理场；面对新设定时，冻结该预训练算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合（compose）局部预测结果，实现从局部学习到全局预测的迁移。

Result: 在两个互补问题上评估：1）稳态Darcy流（大尺度多孔介质中长程压力耦合），相比容量匹配的全域处理模型，适配16个目标模拟后误差降低36-56%；2）围绕俯仰翼型的不稳定不可压缩流（目标俯仰频率超出训练范围时滚动误差累积），在20步场滚动预测中，无论是零样本还是少样本校准后均有改进。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练与组合级迁移是构建物理基础模型的一个有前景的设计原则（design principle）。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [296] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一个流式推测与精炼框架，通过利用部分GPU结果逐步精炼计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，从而在移动和嵌入式设备上高效执行大语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理在隐私和响应性方面有吸引力，但模型权重远超可用DRAM。现有系统利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载到SSD或闪存，但面临根本性系统权衡：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测，导致现有设计要么串行执行，要么产生冗余权重获取、额外计算和大缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream提出"流式推测与精炼"（speculate-and-refine）框架：利用部分GPU计算结果，渐进式地精炼计算、加载和缓存保留的优先级决策，从而在GPU执行与存储I/O之间实现细粒度的重叠，避免早期预测引入的冗余操作并保持决策准确性。

Result: 在移动和嵌入式平台上实现LeanStream后，与先前的设备端LLM推理系统相比，在先前工作达到的最佳吞吐量下，LeanStream将内存用量减少4.8倍至7.5倍，同时将token生成吞吐量进一步提升1.6倍至2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream证明了通过流式精炼而非一次性预测的方式，可以同时解决稀疏执行决策准确性与计算-I/O重叠效率之间的矛盾，在显著降低内存需求的同时提升推理吞吐量。
